Algorithm:
1. Start with a (e.g., uniform or beta shape).
2. Repeat:
   - Compute C=conv(a,a). Find active indices A: C_k >= M - eps*M. Choose a probability p over A (could be uniform or solve for p?).
   - Build linear operator L_p a = conv(p, a)[:n] or matrix. Find a_new such that L_p a_new is as constant as possible and sum=1, a≥0. This can be solved by optimizing a small quadratic: min ||L_p a - 1 μ||^2 + λ(a - a_old)^2, subject sum a=1, a≥0. Use projected gradient / L-BFGS. Since L_p matrix-vector via FFT.
   - Or solve linear system ignoring nonneg: We want L_p a = μ 1. This is a linear system with n+1 variables (a,μ). Least squares. If zeros, clip/normalize.
3. Evaluate, keep best.

Let's implement `subproblem_step(a, C, tau_p, alpha, iters=100)`:
- p = exp(tau_p*(C - maxC)); p /= sum(p). Optionally zero small p<1e-12 and renormalize to keep L approximate.
- Define operator L (full p) with FFT.
- Objective φ(x)=var(Lx)+alpha||x-x0||^2 (var = sum((Lx - mean)^2)). Its gradient: Lx=y; yv=y-y.mean(); g=2*L.T@yv +2*alpha*(x-x0).

```
def subproblem_FISTA(p, x0, alpha=0.05, iters=200, p_mode='fft'):
    n=len(x0)
    # Build operator for p (length 2n-1, nonneg sum1)
    # If p nonzeros K <= max_sparse, build sparse matrix L; else use FFT ops.
```
For sparse build, p may have many small weights; set threshold `p > 1e-10` and renormalize. K likely maybe hundreds. Use `scipy.sparse.csr_matrix`.
Lx: L @ x. LTx: L.T @ y.
For FFT ops, define:
```
def Lx(x): return linear_convolve(p,x)[:n]
def LTx(y):
    N=1; while N < 3*n-2: N<<=1
    Y=fft(y[::-1], N); P=fft(p,N)  # cache P
    out=ifft(Y*P, N).real
    return out[n-1:n-1+n]
```
Cache P for subproblem. For Lx, also can use P*fft(x). Need fft size for p*x length 3n-2. Use N3. For conv C of a*a, use N2=nextpow2(2n-1). Different sizes. We can cache p_fft3.
Objective q(x): y=Lx(x); ym=y-mean; q=sum(ym^2)+alpha*||x-x0||^2.
Grad: 2 L.T@(y-mean) +2alpha(x-x0).
FISTA:

Let's define a local polish `local_polish(a, budget)`:
1. Normalize.
2. Run fixed-p subproblem iterations with adaptive p temperature and alpha decreasing:
   - For cycles: compute C, M. Make p top K or softmax with temperature `temp = M * d`, d from 0.02 to 0.5? Since C scale M/n? M_obj=2nM, M≈0.75/n≈0.00094 for n=800. C max M.    - Run subproblem_FISTA(p, a, alpha) with iters e.g., 150. Alpha decreases from 0.2 to 0.001.
   - Evaluate; if improved, accept; else perturb and continue.

Build p:
```
def make_p(C, spread=0.1):
    M=max(C)
    temp = max(M*spread, 1e-15)
    logp = C/temp - M/temp # = (C-M)/temp
    p = exp(logp); p/=sum
    # optional clip p with min? If p too sparse, L norm small; okay.
    return p
```

```
def conv_self(a):
    n=len(a)
    N=1
    while N < 2*n-1: N <<=1
    fa=rfft(a,N)
    out=irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2*n-1]
    out[np.where(out<0)[0]]=0? small.
    return out
```

```
    N=...
```

`project_simplex(v,s=1)`:
```
v=np.asarray(v,float)
u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
cssv=np.cumsum(u)
rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,len(u)+1)) > (cssv-1))[0][-1]
theta=(cssv[rho]-1)/(rho+1)
return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```

FISTA implementation robust:
```
def subproblem_FISTA(p, x0, alpha=0.1, iters=150, N=None, P_fft=None, verbose=False):
    n=len(x0)
    if P_fft is None:
       N=next_pow2(3*n-2); P_fft=fft(p,N)
    def Lx(x): return ifft(fft(x,N)*P_fft,N).real[:n]
    def LTy(y): return ifft(fft(y[::-1],N)*P_fft,N).real[n-1:n-1+n]
    x_best=x0.copy(); q_best = None
    y=x0.copy(); t=1.0
    x=x0.copy()
    step=1/(2*(1+alpha)+1e-12)
    for it in range(iters):
       grad_y = ...
       x_new = project_simplex(y - step*grad_y)
       q_new = phi(x_new)
       # Monotone check: q_old_y = phi(y)? y may infeasible; but phi defined. We want decrease from y. If q_new > phi(y) - ... reduce step? For FISTA, restart if q_new > q_y.
       q_y = phi(y) # expensive
       if q_new > q_y + 1e-12:
           # reduce step and use gradient at x? maybe reset
           step *= 0.5
           # try simple step from x
           g = grad(x)
           x_new = project_simplex(x - step*g)
           q_new=phi(x_new)
           if q_new > phi(x):
             break
       # momentum
       t_new = 0.5*(1+sqrt(1+4*t*t))
       y = project_simplex(x_new + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x_new - x))
       x = x_new; t=t_new
       if q_new < q_best: best=x_new
       if it%50==0 and verbose: print
       step = min(step*1.02, 1/(2*(1+alpha)))
    return best, q_best
```
This may be slow computing phi(y) each iter (2 Lx). But n=800, iters 150, okay. We can optimize by only compute q_new for x_new; for restart check maintain q_y from previous? In FISTA, y not feasible, but phi(y) can be computed. Fine.

But if p full dense, each Lx uses FFT length8192. 150* maybe 4 FFT =600 FFT*8192 ~5M operations, fine. Multiple subproblems maybe 50 ->25M, okay in 1500s? Python FFT 8192 ~0.1ms? Actually maybe 0.5ms, 25M ~12s? plus overhead okay.

Use `scipy.fft.fft` can be fast.

But `phi` requires `Lx` and mean; grad requires LTy. Each loop 2 Lx? For grad_y: yv=Lx(y)-mean; Lt_y=Lty(yv). q_y=Lx(y) again? We can store yv from previous? Structure:
At end of iteration, set y = new y; for next, compute Lx(y). We can keep cache but no.
Implement:
```
def phi(x, return_y=False):
   y=Lx(x); ym=y-y.mean(); return float(ym@ym)+alpha*np.sum((x-x0)**2)
```

`make_p_from_C(C, mode, spread, K)`:
- Normalize C? C max M. If M=0 p uniform.
- mode 0 softmax exp((C-M)/ (M*spread)) ; if spread=0 use uniform top K.
- mode 1 top K uniform: idx=np.argsort(C)[-K:]; p[idx]=1/K.
- mode 2 Gaussian band centered at argmax with width = width_frac*n: p=exp(-((idx-center)^2)/(2σ^2)), renorm.
- mode 3 mixture broad: p=(exp((C-M)/(M*spread))+exp(-abs(idx-center)/(σ)))/(2)

```
def perturb(a,rng,method,scale):
  n=len(a)
  if method==0: noise=rng.normal(0,scale,n); a=a*np.exp(noise)
  elif 1: shift=np.roll(a, int(rng.integers(-n//20,n//20)))
  elif 2: # sinusoidal
     m=int(rng.integers(1,6)); phase=rng.uniform(0,2pi); amp=rng.uniform(0,scale*5)
     x=np.linspace(0,1,n); a=a*(1+amp*np.sin(2pi*m*x+phase))
  elif 3: # block noise
     m=8; a_block flat? 
  normalize, clip
```

Potential bug: `Lx` via FFT of p (length 2n-1) and x (length n) gives cyclic convolution length N. Need N≥3n-2 to avoid wrap. Good. The result first n is `∑_{j=0}^{n-1} p_{i+j} x_j`? Let's verify convolution index: y_m=∑_{t=0}^{lenp-1} p_t x_{m-t}. We want for i: ∑_{j} p_{i+j} x_j. Let t=i+j, m=i. Then t=m? Not sum over t? y_i =∑_j p_{i-j?} x_j depending definition. The linear convolution `conv(p,x)[i] = ∑_{j} p[i-j] x[j]` (standard); we need p[i+j]. That's cross-correlation, not standard convolution! I made an error. Important! Derivative gradient q_i =∑_{k} p_k a_{k-i} =∑_{j} p_{i+j} a_j. This is not standard conv(p,a), but correlation of p with a. Need compute correctly.

Let's fix. For p length 2n-1, a length n. We want q_i = ∑_{j=0}^{n-1} p_{i+j} a_j. This is the "upper" part of a Hankel multiplication. This is not `np.convolve(p,a)[:n]`? Let's test n=2: p=[p0,p1,p2], a=[a0,a1]. np.convolve(p,a) = [p0a0, p0a1+p1a0, p1a1+p2a0, p2a1]. First 2: [p0a0, p0a1+p1a0] = desired [p0a0, p0a1+p1a0]? desired i=1: p1a0+p2a1. Missing p2a1, includes p0a1. So not correct. Need cross-correlation.

We want q_i = ∑_j p_{i+j} a_j. Let reverse a? Define ar = a[::-1] length n. Convolve p with ar: [m] =∑_t p_t ar_{m-t}. ar_{n-1-j}=a_j. Need m? For q_i, terms p_{i+j} a_j = p_t ar_{n-1-j} with t=i+j, ar index = n-1-j. t+ index = i+j + n-1-j = n-1+i. So q_i = (p * ar)[n-1+i]. Great. So Lx = conv(p, a_rev)[n-1:n-1+n]. Similarly LTy = L^T y_j =∑_i p_{i+j} y_i. Let a? This is similar: use y reversed? We want r_j=∑_i p_{i+j} y_i. Let y_r = y[::-1], y_r[n-1-i]=y_i. p * y_r index = i? p_t y_r_{M-t}. Want t=i+j, y_r index=n-1-i. Sum t+idx = i+j+n-1-i = n-1+j. So r_j = conv(p, y_r)[n-1+j]. It is the same operator! Both Lx and LTy use convolution of p with reversed vector and extract [n-1:n-1+n]. Nice. So define:
```
def L_op(vec):
    vrev = vec[::-1]
    out = lin_conv(p, vrev)[n-1:n-1+n]
    return out
```
This O(N) FFT. For gradient of C: g_i = 2 * L_op(a)_i. Let's test n=2: p*ar=[p0a1, p0a0+p1a1, p1a0+p2a1, p2a0]. Extract index1..2: [p0a0+p1a1, p1a0+p2a1]. Desired derivative wrt a0: 2(p0a0 +? C2 p2? derivative wrt a0 includes p0 and? p? For p1 (C1=2a0a1) derivative wrt a0 2a1 p1; formula gives p0a0 + p1a1 yes. Wrappers. Good.

```
    g=2*L_op(p,a)
    for tries in range(10):
       new=project_simplex(a - step*g)
       obj=compute_obj(new)
       if obj < compute_obj(a): return new,obj,step
       step*=0.5
    return a, compute_obj(a), step
```

Now, `project_simplex` implementation edge cases: If v all -inf? not. Use sorted descending.
```
def project_simplex(v, s=1.0):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=float).copy()
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,len(u)+1)) > (cssv - s))[0]
    if len(rho)==0: return np.zeros_like(v)
    rho=rho[-1]
    theta=(cssv[rho]-s)/(rho+1)
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
```
Check for s=1.

```
def simple_parametric_starts(n, budget, rng=None):
  starts=[]
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  shapes=[]
  shapes.append(np.ones(n))
  for beta in [-3,-2,-1,-0.5,0.5,1,2,3]: shapes.append(np.exp(beta*x))
  for p in [0.25,0.5,1,2,3]: shapes.append(x**p); shapes.append((1-x+1e-6)**p); shapes.append(np.minimum(x,1-x)**p+1e-6)
  for A in [0.3,0.6,0.9]: for phase in [0, np.pi/2, np.pi, 3*np.pi/2]: shapes.append(1+A*np.sin(2*np.pi*x+phase))
  # maybe polynomial
  for c1 in [-1,1]: for c2 in [-1,1]: shapes.append(1+c1*(2*x-1)+c2*(2*x-1)**2)
  evaluate, normalize, positive.
  keep unique best maybe.
```

`local_search` code in detail:
```
def local_search(a, budget, deadline, rng, tag=''):
    n=len(a); a=normalize(a); best=a.copy(); best_obj=compute_objective(a)
    it_outer=0; no_imp=0
    p_cache={}
    while time.monotonic() < min(deadline, start+budget):
       C=conv_self(a); M=C.max(); obj=2*n*M
       if obj < best_obj: best...; no_imp=0
       else: no_imp += 1? (but use best separately)
       # if very close? still continue
       spreads = [0.05, 0.2, 0.7]
       Ks = [20, 80, 300]
       # choose 3 p per outer: soft0.05, topK20, soft0.4? Maybe random.
       for mode in range(3):
           if time... break
           if mode==0: p=make_p_soft(C, 0.05 + 0.03*(it_outer%5))
           elif mode==1: p=make_p_top(C, max(10, min(300, 20 + it_outer*10)))
           else: p=make_p_band(C, 0.15 + 0.05*(it_outer%5))
           alpha = [0.2,0.05,0.01][mode] * (0.85 ** it_outer)
           alpha = max(alpha, 1e-4)
           p_fft,N = make_L_op_cache(p,n)
           cand = subproblem_FISTA(p_fft,N,p,a,alpha,iters=60)
           improved=False
           for beta in [1.0,0.75,0.5,0.25,0.1]:
               # if cand same as a skip?
               b=blend(a,cand,beta)
               ob=compute_objective(b)
               if ob < best_obj - 1e-12:
                   best_obj=ob; best=b.copy(); a=b.copy(); improved=True; break
           if improved: no_imp=0; break? maybe continue with current a
       if not improved:
           no_imp += 1
           # try active step on best/a
           if no_imp > 1:
                C=conv_self(a)
                for K in [10,50,200]:
                   p=make_p_top(C,K)
                   p_fft,N=...
                   g=2*L_op_from_cache(p_fft,N,a,n) # top-level? Need L_op not subproblem
                   # backtracking
                   step=0.25
                   for tries in range(8):
                      new=project_simplex(a - step*g)
                      ob=compute_objective(new)
                      if ob<best_obj:
                         best_obj=ob; best=new.copy(); a=new.copy(); no_imp=0; improved2=True; break
                      step*=0.5
                if improved2: break
           if no_imp > 6:
                # perturb best
                method = int(rng.integers(0,5))
                scale=0.02 + 0.01*(it_outer%5)
                a=perturb_sequence(best, rng, method, scale)
                best_obj=compute_objective(best) # keep best
                # set a to perturbation, but best unchanged
                no_imp=0
       it_outer +=1
       if it_outer > 200: break
    return best,best_obj
```
Need top-level `apply_L(p_fft,N,a,n)`.

```
def subproblem_FISTA(p_fft,N,p,x0,alpha,iters=60,verbose=False):
    n=len(x0)
    best=x0.copy()
    y=x0.copy()
    x=x0.copy()
    t=1.0
    step=1.0/(2.0*(1.0+alpha)+1e-12)
    def phi?? can't closure. We'll compute inline.
    for it in range(iters):
       # compute Lx_y
       yL = apply_L(p_fft,N,y,n)
       ymean = yL.mean()
       yv = yL - ymean
       grad_y = 2.0*(apply_L(p_fft,N,yv,n) + alpha*(y-x0))
       x_new = project_simplex(y - step*grad_y)
       # compute q_new
       xL_new = apply_L(p_fft,N,x_new,n); xm_new=xL_new.mean(); xv=xL_new-xm_new
       q_new = float(np.dot(xv,xv)) + alpha*float(np.dot(x_new-x0,x_new-x0))
       # compute q_y maybe if needed
       # optional q_y from yL, yv and y feasible? y may not feasible, alpha term
       q_y = float(np.dot(yv,yv)) + alpha*float(np.dot(y-x0,y-x0))
       if q_new > q_y + 1e-10*max(1.0,q_y):
           # restart: use previous x, maybe reduce step
           t=1.0
           # try gradient from x
           # compute grad_x? expensive; just set y=x
           y=x
           step *= 0.7
           if step < 1e-6: break
           continue
       t_new = 0.5*(1.0 + math.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0*t*t))
       y_next = x_new + ((t-1.0)/t_new)*(x_new - x)
       # keep y maybe not feasible; no projection needed? For gradient OK. But project to avoid large? Could project y_next to simplex to control.
       # Projected FISTA typically y can be infeasible; gradient fine. But L_op on large outside maybe okay.
       x = x_new; t=t_new; y=y_next
       if q_new < q_best: best=x_new.copy(); q_best=q_new
       step = min(step*1.01, 1.0/(2.0*(1.0+alpha)+1e-12))
    return best
```

```
def low_dim_hill_climb(n, c0, budget, rng):
```

Implementation of `resample_sequence`:
```
def resample_sequence(a, new_n):
    n=len(a)
    if n==new_n: return a.copy()
    if n==1: return np.full(new_n,a[0])
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
    new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    return np.maximum(new,0)
```
If a has zeros at endpoints, interpolation fine.

`normalize`:
```
def normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12: return np.ones_like(a)/len(a)
    return a/s
```
If a empty? no.

`clip_to_1000`? We return normalized <=1, no need.

Let's refine `local_search` to use time budget correctly:
```
def local_search(a, budget, deadline, rng, tag=''):
    start=time.monotonic(); cutoff=min(deadline, start+budget)
    a=normalize(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_obj=compute_objective(a)
    outer=0; no_imp=0
    while time.monotonic() < cutoff:
       C=conv_self(a); M=float(C.max()); obj=2*len(a)*M
       improved_any=False
       if obj < best_obj:
          best_obj=obj; best=a.copy(); no_imp=0; improved_any=True
       else:
          no_imp += 1
       # subproblem attempts
       if no_imp > 20:
           # perturb best but a maybe current
           a=perturb_sequence(best,rng,int(rng.integers(0,5)),0.02+0.01*(outer%4)); no_imp=0
       p_specs = [(0,0.05+0.02*(outer%5)), (1, max(10,min(400,20+15*outer))), (2,0.15+0.04*(outer%5))]
       alphas = [max(0.2*(0.9**outer),0.005), max(0.05*(0.9**outer),0.002), max(0.01*(0.9**outer),0.001)]
       for idx in range(3):
           if time.monotonic() >= cutoff: break
           mode, param = p_specs[idx]
           if mode==0: p=make_p_soft(C,param)
           elif mode==1: p=make_p_top(C,int(param))
           else: p=make_p_band(C,param)
           alpha=alphas[idx]
           p_fft,N=make_L_op_cache(p,len(a))
           cand=subproblem_FISTA(p_fft,N,p,a,alpha,iters=50)
           # evaluate blends
           improved=False
           for beta in [1.0,0.75,0.5,0.3]:
               b=blend(a,cand,beta)
               ob=compute_objective(b)
               if ob < best_obj - 1e-12:
                  best_obj=ob; best=b.copy(); a=b.copy(); improved=True; improved_any=True; break
           if improved:
               no_imp=0
               C=conv_self(a) # update p for next modes? Could break and use new C.
               # break to recompute p from improved a
               break
       if not improved_any:
           # active step
           step=0.2
           for K in [10,50,200]:
              if time... break
              p=make_p_top(C,K)
              p_fft,N=make_L_op_cache(p,len(a))
              g=2.0*apply_L(p_fft,N,a,len(a))
              for tries in range(8):
                 new=project_simplex(a-step*g)
                 ob=compute_objective(new)
                 if ob < best_obj:
                    best_obj=ob; best=new.copy(); a=new.copy(); improved_any=True; break
                 step*=0.5
              if improved_any: break
           if not improved_any and no_imp>8:
              a=perturb_sequence(best,rng,int(rng.integers(0,5)),0.02+0.01*(outer%4)); no_imp=0
       outer+=1
    return best,best_obj
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2
using a combination of:
- FFT convolution objective
- low-dimensional random/sinusoidal parametric starts
- convex proximal-style subproblems for active-lag probability vectors p:
    minimize Var(L_p a) + alpha ||a-a0||^2
  where (L_p a)_i = sum_j p_{i+j} a_j
- projected gradient steps on weighted active convolution terms
- multi-length resampling and local polishing
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft, rfft, irfft


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    x = 1
    while x < m:
        x <<= 1
    return x


def normalize_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / s


def conv_self(a):
    """Full linear convolution a*a by FFT."""
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    fa = rfft(a, N)
    out = irfft(fa * fa, N)[:2 * n - 1]
    out = np.where(out > 0.0, out, 0.0)
    return out


def obj_from_conv(C, n):
    if C.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    return 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(C))


def compute_objective(a):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    C = conv_self(a)
    return obj_from_conv(C, a.size)


def blend_sequences(a, b, beta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if b.size != a.size:
        b = resample_sequence(b, a.size)
    z = (1.0 - beta) * a + beta * b
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = np.maximum(z, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(z))
    if s < 1e-12:
        return normalize_sequence(a)
    return z / s


def resample_sequence(a, new_n):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    new_n = int(max(1, new_n))
    if n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    if n == 1:
        return np.full(new_n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_sequence(y)


def project_simplex(v, s=1.0):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, -1e12)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.nonzero(u * (np.arange(1, len(u) + 1)) > (cssv - s))[0]
    if rho.size == 0:
        return np.zeros_like(v)
    rho = rho[-1]
    theta = (cssv[rho] - s) / (rho + 1.0)
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)


def make_L_op_cache(p, n):
    """FFT cache for the symmetric L_p operator: (L_p x)_i = sum_j p_{i+j} x_j."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s < 1e-14:
        p = np.ones(max(1, 2 * n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
        p /= p.size
    else:
        p = p / s
    N = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    return fft(p, N), N


def apply_L(p_fft, N, x, n):
    """Apply L_p to x using cached FFT of p."""
    xr = x[::-1].copy()
    out = ifft(fft(xr, N) * p_fft, N).real
    return out[n - 1:n - 1 + n]


def subproblem_FISTA(p_fft, N, p, x0, alpha, iters=60, verbose=False):
    """
    Approximate minimization of
        Var(L_p x) + alpha ||x-x0||^2
    over the simplex.
    """
    n = x0.size
    x0 = normalize_sequence(x0)
    best = x0.copy()
    xL0 = apply_L(p_fft, N, x0, n)
    xv0 = xL0 - xL0.mean()
    q_best = float(np.dot(xv0, xv0)) + 1e-18

    x = x0.copy()
    y = x0.copy()
    t = 1.0
    step = 1.0 / (2.0 * (1.0 + alpha) + 1e-12)
    stagnant = 0

    for it in range(iters):
        yL = apply_L(p_fft, N, y, n)
        ymean = yL.mean()
        yv = yL - ymean

        grad_y = 2.0 * (apply_L(p_fft, N, yv, n) + alpha * (y - x0))
        cand = project_simplex(y - step * grad_y)

        candL = apply_L(p_fft, N, cand, n)
        candv = candL - candL.mean()
        q_cand = float(np.dot(candv, candv)) + alpha * float(np.dot(cand - x0, cand - x0))

        if not math.isfinite(q_cand):
            break

        if q_cand < q_best - 1e-15:
            q_best = q_cand
            best = cand.copy()
            stagnant = 0
        else:
            stagnant += 1

        # Mild monotonicity/restart check.
        q_y = float(np.dot(yv, yv)) + alpha * float(np.dot(y - x0, y - x0))
        if q_cand > q_y + 1e-9 * max(1.0, abs(q_y)):
            t = 1.0
            y = x.copy()
            step *= 0.75
            if step < 1e-8:
                break
        else:
            t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + math.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t))
            y_next = cand + ((t - 1.0) / t_new) * (cand - x)
            y = project_simplex(y_next)
            t = t_new

        x = cand
        step = min(step * 1.01, 1.0 / (2.0 * (1.0 + alpha) + 1e-12))

        if stagnant > 35:
            break

        if verbose and it % 25 == 0:
            print(f"      subproblem it={it} q={q_best:.12f} step={step:.6f}")

    return best


def make_p_soft(C, spread):
    M = float(np.max(C))
    if M <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(C.size, dtype=np.float64) / C.size
    temp = max(1e-15, M * max(1e-3, float(spread)))
    logp = (C - M) / temp
    p = np.exp(logp)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s < 1e-14:
        idx = int(np.argmax(C))
        p = np.zeros(C.size, dtype=np.float64)
        p[idx] = 1.0
        return p
    return p / s


def make_p_top(C, K):
    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=np.float64)
    K = int(max(1, min(C.size, K)))
    idx = np.argsort(C)[-K:]
    p = np.zeros(C.size, dtype=np.float64)
    p[idx] = 1.0 / K
    return p


def make_p_band(C, frac):
    n = C.size
    center = int(np.argmax(C))
    sigma = max(1.0, frac * n)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.exp(-0.5 * ((idx - center) / sigma) ** 2)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s < 1e-14:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return p / s


def perturb_sequence(a, rng, method, scale):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    scale = float(scale)
    b = a.copy()

    if method == 0:
        mult = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5 * scale, n))
        b = a * mult
    elif method == 1:
        m = int(rng.integers(1, 9))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.0)) * min(1.0, scale * 10.0)
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        b = a * (1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + phase))
    elif method == 2:
        shift = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 20 + 1)))
        b = np.roll(a, shift)
    elif method == 3:
        block = int(rng.integers(4, 17))
        mult = rng.uniform(0.6, 1.4, block)
        idx = (np.arange(n) * block) // n
        b = a * mult[idx]
    else:
        b = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25 * scale, n))

    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return normalize_sequence(b)


def simple_parametric_starts(n, deadline, rng=None):
    out = []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    shapes = [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)]

    for beta in (-4.0, -2.5, -1.5, -0.7, 0.7, 1.5, 2.5, 4.0):
        shapes.append(np.exp(beta * x))

    for p in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
        shapes.append(x ** p)
        shapes.append(np.power(np.maximum(1.0 - x, 1e-8), p))
        shapes.append(np.power(x * (1.0 - x) + 1e-6, p))

    for m in (1, 2, 3):
        for A in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 0.95):
            for phase in (0.0, np.pi / 4.0, np.pi / 2.0, 3.0 * np.pi / 4.0,
                          np.pi, 5.0 * np.pi / 4.0, 3.0 * np.pi / 2.0, 7.0 * np.pi / 4.0):
                shapes.append(1.0 + A * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + phase))

    shapes.append(1.0 + 0.8 * (2.0 * x - 1.0))
    shapes.append(1.0 - 0.8 * (2.0 * x - 1.0))
    shapes.append(1.0 + 0.5 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) + 0.3 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)
    shapes.append(1.0 - 0.5 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) - 0.3 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)

    for sh in shapes:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        a = normalize_sequence(sh)
        C = conv_self(a)
        ob = obj_from_conv(C, n)
        if math.isfinite(ob):
            out.append((ob, a))
    return out


def low_dim_random_starts(n, num_samples, deadline, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    modes = (1, 2, 3, 5)
    sin_basis = []
    cos_basis = []
    for m in modes:
        th = 2.0 * np.pi * m * x
        sin_basis.append(np.sin(th))
        cos_basis.append(np.cos(th))
    md = len(modes)

    best_a = None
    best_ob = np.inf

    for _ in range(num_samples):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        cs = rng.uniform(-0.8, 0.8, md)
        cp = rng.uniform(-0.35, 0.35, md)
        u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(md):
            u += cs[j] * sin_basis[j] + cp[j] * cos_basis[j]
        amp = float(np.max(np.abs(u)))
        if amp > 0.9:
            u *= 0.9 / amp
        w = 1.0 + u
        w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
        w = np.maximum(w, 0.0)
        s = float(np.sum(w))
        if s < 1e-12:
            continue
        a = w / s
        C = conv_self(a)
        ob = obj_from_conv(C, n)
        if ob < best_ob:
            best_ob = ob
            best_a = a.copy()

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = normalize_sequence(np.ones(n))
        best_ob = compute_objective(best_a)
    return best_a, best_ob


def local_search(a, budget, deadline, rng, tag=""):
    start = time.monotonic()
    cutoff = min(deadline, start + max(1.0, float(budget)))

    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    best = a.copy()
    best_obj = compute_objective(a)
    print(f"  [{tag}] start obj={best_obj:.8f} n={n}")

    outer = 0
    no_imp = 0

    while time.monotonic() < cutoff:
        if outer % 5 == 0:
            print(f"  [{tag}] outer={outer} obj={best_obj:.8f} no_imp={no_imp}")

        C = conv_self(a)
        obj = obj_from_conv(C, n)
        improved_any = False

        if obj < best_obj - 1e-13:
            best_obj = obj
            best = a.copy()
            no_imp = 0
            improved_any = True
        else:
            no_imp += 1

        # If badly stuck, perturb from best.
        if no_imp > 25:
            a = perturb_sequence(best, rng, int(rng.integers(0, 4)), 0.03 + 0.01 * (outer % 4))
            no_imp = 0
            C = conv_self(a)
            obj = obj_from_conv(C, n)

        # Run a few proximal convex subproblems with different p.
        alpha1 = max(0.004, 0.18 * (0.88 ** outer))
        alpha2 = max(0.0015, 0.05 * (0.88 ** outer))
        alpha3 = max(0.0007, 0.012 * (0.88 ** outer))

        p_specs = [
            (0, 0.04 + 0.02 * (outer % 7), alpha1),
            (1, int(max(12, min(450, 24 + 18 * outer))), alpha2),
            (2, 0.14 + 0.05 * (outer % 5), alpha3),
        ]

        improved_local = False

        for mode, param, alpha in p_specs:
            if time.monotonic() >= cutoff:
                break

            if mode == 0:
                p = make_p_soft(C, param)
            elif mode == 1:
                p = make_p_top(C, int(param))
            else:
                p = make_p_band(C, float(param))

            p_fft, N = make_L_op_cache(p, n)
            cand = subproblem_FISTA(p_fft, N, p, a, alpha, iters=55)

            improved = False
            for beta in (1.0, 0.75, 0.55, 0.35, 0.18):
                b = blend_sequences(a, cand, beta)
                ob = compute_objective(b)
                if math.isfinite(ob) and ob < best_obj - 1e-13:
                    best_obj = ob
                    best = b.copy()
                    a = b.copy()
                    improved = True
                    improved_any = True
                    improved_local = True
                    print(f"    [{tag}] improved via subproblem mode={mode} beta={beta:.2f} obj={best_obj:.8f}")
                    break

            if improved:
                C = conv_self(a)
                break

        if not improved_any:
            # Direct active weighted-gradient step with backtracking on true objective.
            for K in (12, 48, 190):
                if time.monotonic() >= cutoff:
                    break
                p = make_p_top(C, K)
                p_fft, N = make_L_op_cache(p, n)
                g = 2.0 * apply_L(p_fft, N, a, n)
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
                    continue

                step = 0.18
                for _ in range(10):
                    b = project_simplex(a - step * g)
                    ob = compute_objective(b)
                    if math.isfinite(ob) and ob < best_obj - 1e-13:
                        best_obj = ob
                        best = b.copy()
                        a = b.copy()
                        improved_any = True
                        print(f"    [{tag}] active step improved K={K} obj={best_obj:.8f}")
                        break
                    step *= 0.55
                if improved_any:
                    break

            if not improved_any and no_imp > 10:
                a = perturb_sequence(best, rng, int(rng.integers(0, 5)), 0.02 + 0.012 * (outer % 5))
                no_imp = 0

        outer += 1
        if outer > 250:
            break

    print(f"  [{tag}] local done obj={best_obj:.8f}")
    return best, best_obj


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget - 8.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent_arr = None
    parent_n = 800
    try:
        pa = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if pa.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(pa)):
            pa = np.maximum(pa, 0.0)
            if pa.sum() > 1e-12:
                parent_arr = pa
                parent_n = pa.size
    except Exception:
        parent_arr = None

    if parent_n < 350 or parent_n > 2200:
        n_main = 800
    else:
        n_main = int(parent_n)

    print(f"Main length n={n_main}, budget={budget_s:.0f}s")

    starts = []
    counter = 0

    if parent_arr is not None:
        a = resample_sequence(parent_arr, n_main)
        ob = compute_objective(a)
        starts.append((ob, counter, a))
        counter += 1
        print(f"Parent resampled obj={ob:.8f}")

    a = normalize_sequence(np.ones(n_main))
    ob = compute_objective(a)
    starts.append((ob, counter, a))
    counter += 1

    init_end = t0 + min(110.0, max(10.0, (deadline - t0) * 0.12))

    param_starts = simple_parametric_starts(n_main, init_end, rng)
    for ob, a in param_starts:
        starts.append((ob, counter, a))
        counter += 1
    if param_starts:
        best_p = min(param_starts, key=lambda z: z[0])  # only small list; lambda is acceptable? We avoid; use min explicit below.
    # To avoid lambda entirely:
    if param_starts:
        bp_obj = np.inf
        for ob, a in param_starts:
            if ob < bp_obj:
                bp_obj = ob
        print(f"Best parametric start obj={bp_obj:.8f}")

    a, ob = low_dim_random_starts(n_main, num_samples=1400, deadline=init_end, rng=rng)
    starts.append((ob, counter, a))
    counter += 1
    print(f"Low-dim random start obj={ob:.8f}")

    # Sort by objective without lambda: tuple first two values are comparable.
    starts.sort()

    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf

    # Pick top distinct-ish starts for main length.
    top_starts = []
    seen_patterns = 0
    for ob, cnt, a in starts:
        if len(top_starts) >= 4:
            break
        top_starts.append(a)

    # Main length optimization.
    for i, a in enumerate(top_starts):
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 80:
            break
        alloc = min(remaining * 0.32, 560.0)
        if i == 0:
            alloc = min(remaining * 0.5, 1000.0)
        a2, ob2 = local_search(a, alloc, deadline, rng, tag=f"main{i}")
        if ob2 < best_score:
            best_score = ob2
            best_seq = a2.copy()

    # Optional multi-length resampling.
    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = normalize_sequence(np.ones(n_main))
        best_score = compute_objective(best_seq)

    cur_n = len(best_seq)
    n_candidates = []
    for dx in (0, 80, 200, 420, -120, -260, 650):
        nn = cur_n + dx
        if 450 <= nn <= 2000:
            n_candidates.append(int(nn))
    for nn in (700, 900, 1100, 1400, 1800):
        if nn not in n_candidates:
            n_candidates.append(nn)

    # Remove duplicates while preserving order.
    unique_ns = []
    for nn in n_candidates:
        if nn not in unique_ns:
            unique_ns.append(nn)

    for tri, nn in enumerate(unique_ns[:5]):
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 120:
            break
        if nn == len(best_seq):
            continue
        a_try = resample_sequence(best_seq, nn)
        alloc = min(remaining * 0.20, 380.0)
        a2, ob2 = local_search(a_try, alloc, deadline, rng, tag=f"n{nn}")
        if ob2 < best_score:
            best_score = ob2
            best_seq = a2.copy()
            print(f"Multi-n improved n={nn} obj={best_score:.8f}")

    # Final polish on current best sequence.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 50:
        a2, ob2 = local_search(best_seq, min(remaining * 0.45, 360.0), deadline, rng, tag="final")
        if ob2 < best_score:
            best_score = ob2
            best_seq = a2.copy()

    if best_seq is None or len(best_seq) == 0:
        best_seq = np.ones(100, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = compute_objective(best_seq)

    best_seq = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64)
    best_seq = np.where(np.isfinite(best_seq), best_seq, 0.0)
    best_seq = np.maximum(best_seq, 0.0)
    best_seq = best_seq / max(1e-15, float(np.sum(best_seq)))

    result = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_seq]

    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(result)
        if math.isfinite(val):
            best_score = val
    except Exception:
        val = np.inf

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    print(f"FINAL n={len(result)} internal={2*len(result)*float(np.max(conv_self(normalize_sequence(result)))):.8f} evaluated={val:.8f} time={elapsed:.1f}s")

    if not math.isfinite(val):
        # Fallback that is always valid.
        result = [1.0] * 100

    return result
```
